    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. 的行为，发现 K*=2 在所有 8 个测试条件下保持不变，展现出跨模型架构、跨解码策略、跨生成模式的 universality。\\n\\n**子组件：**\\n\\n**(a) K*=2 universality across architectures, generation modes, and decoding strategies**\\n> **4/4 model-mode 1v2 range [0.6866, 0.7039]，K* 完全不变\\n> - K*=2 在所有 8 个测试条件下保持不变（4 model-mode configurations + 4 decoding strategies）\\n> - ⚠️ θ-sensitivity: θ=0.60 下 4/4 K*=2；θ=0.70 下仅 Config4-T12 K*=2（1v2=0.7039 仅超 0.70 阈值 0.004），其余 3 config K*=1。论文以 θ=0.60 为主结论，θ=0.70 边界效应作 footnote\\n> - ⚠️ 特征排序分化: K* 跨 config 稳定，但特征重要性排序不完全一致——Greedy vs T1.2 Spearman=0.53, p=0.041（Bonferroni 校正后阈值 0.0083，不显著）。论文中措辞应为 \\\"suggestive\\\" 而非确定性结论。Config4-T12 正确 top-3 features: hapax_ratio, ttr, surp_skew（非 freq_entropy）\\n> - ⚠️ K*=2 边缘性双端注意: Qwen-cont 1v2 CI lower=0.6015（+0.15pp 高于 θ）+ Qwen-rw 2v3 CI upper=0.5991（-0.09pp 低于 θ），K*=2 边界在两端都极边缘。Discussion/Limitations 需同时提及\\n> - Paper 可写：\\\"K*=2 is established as a consistent discrimination boundary across all 8 tested conditions (4 model-mode configurations and 4 decoding strategies; bootstrap 95% CI for AUC₁ᵥ₂ > 0.60; AUC₂ᵥ₃ CI upper < 0.60), suggesting universality beyond the ≤1.5B scale studied.\\\"\\n> - ⚠️ 措辞策略（D017 Reviewer 评估）：分两层——\\\"established as consistent\\\" 用确定性语言（8/8 有统计检验支撑），\\\"suggesting universality beyond\\\" 对外推 hedging。不升级为 \\\"establishing universal\\\"——3 模型 ≤1.5B 会引发审稿人质疑消耗 rebuttal 预算\\n> - ⚠️ C1' 子组件定位：呈现为\\\"对同一 claim 的 4 个 evidence 维度\\\"而非 4 个独立 finding。§4 标题用 \\\"K* Characterization\\\"，每个子节开头显式回答一个 reviewer concern，子节间用 transitional sentences 链接\\n\\n**(b) Cross-model transfer**\\n> - Pythia↔OLMo 90-92% accuracy retention → K*=2 不是 model self-familiarity artifact\\n> - 0v1 AUC > 0.96 across all transfer pairs → binary contamination signal 完全跨模型可迁移\\n> - 1v2+ transfer 取决于模型族相似度 → 与 K*=2 完美一致（K* 之上分辨力本就微弱）\\n> - transfer retention 80-92% suggests depth signal has a large text-intrinsic component（不量化具体比例，因 transfer accuracy retention ≠ 信号分解）\\n> - ⚠️ 数值精度：Pythia→OLMo 91.6%, OLMo→Pythia 90.3%，范围 90-92%（非 91-92%）；Qwen-cont↔Pythia 84.7%/85.2%，范围 84-85%（非 83-85%）\\n\\n**(c) Degradation ordering consistency**\\n> - Spearman ρ ≥ 0.85 on single-feature AUC ranking（mode-controlled, continuation）\\n> - Top discriminative features shared: surp_d1_std, surp_std, surp_d2_std\\n> - Limitation: depth-pair level 一致性在 1v2 处较弱（τ~0.37）\\n> - Metric: single-feature AUC ranking（model-free, not LightGBM split count）\\n\\n**(e) Representation dependence of K* (§4.x Neural Probe / §5.x Discussion)**\\n> - exp-015: frozen Qwen-1.5B embeddings (1536-dim) + MLP probe → K*(neural)=3 on Qwen-cont（2v3 AUC=0.6195, fold range [0.6052, 0.6323]）\\n> - K* hierarchy: K*_theory ≥ K*_neural ≥ K*_15D，证明 K* 是表示复杂度的函数，K*_15D=2 是操作下界而非信息论极限\\n> - Interpretability–discriminability tradeoff: 15D features（K*=2, model-free, interpretable, deployable）vs neural probe（K*=3, model-dependent, opaque, 100x+ compute）\\n> - ⚠️ 当前仅 Qwen-cont 1 个数据集（Pythia/OLMo 待重跑）。若 Pythia/OLMo K*_neural=2 → self-familiarity 效应（Qwen embeddings 对 Qwen 生成文本更敏感），反而加强 15D model-free features 的定位\\n> - ⚠️ 2v3 边界极度边缘（fold 2 仅 +0.52pp 超 θ），需 bootstrap CI + permutation test 统计确认（重跑脚本已含）\\n> - 论文定位：**深化 K* characterization**（不推翻 K*=2 headline）。Headline 保持 \\\"K*_empirical=2 under 15D hand-crafted features\\\"，新增子结论 \\\"K*_neural=3 demonstrates representation-dependent boundary\\\"\\n> - Discussion 结构：§5.1 现有内容保留 + §5.1bis \\\"Representation Dependence of K*\\\" 独立子节（积极发现，非 limitation）\\n> - 预防审稿人 \\\"why bury the stronger result?\\\"：K*_15D=2 是 practical/deployable boundary，K*_neural=3 是 theoretical exploration，两者服务不同目的\\n\\n**(d) Generation mode analysis (§4.x Analysis subsection)**\\n> - Signal strength: rewrite AUC 1v2=0.6957 vs continuation 0.6114（Δ=+8.4pp）\\n> - Transferability: continuation 80-92% retention vs rewrite 61-63%\\n> - Feature subset: cont→rw 89% > rw→cont 82% → continuation features 是 rewrite 的通用子集\\n> - Interpretation: rewrite mode 的 instruction-following 引入 mode-specific style artifacts，放大 depth signal 但降低跨模型泛化性\\n> - 论文定位：Analysis subsection（\\\"How does generation mode affect depth estimation?\\\"），不是独立 claim\\n\\n### Secondary: C3 (Graduated Filtering)\\n即使在 K*=2 的粗粒度 ordinal signal（human/gen-1/gen-2+ 三级）下，K*-aware graduated filtering 仍优于 binary filtering，因为 ordinal signal 在阈值附近提供更 calibrated 的质量估计。\\n\\n### Supporting Analysis: Canonical Condition Selection\\nGeneration mode 是 depth estimation 的显著混淆变量。Methodology 段落阐述 continuation mode 作为 canonical condition 的选择及理由（不作为独立 claim）。\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: Web 数据正被多代递归合成文本污染。当前检测方法将合成文本视为 binary 类别（real vs synthetic），但质量随代际深度非线性退化——首代合成文本与四代合成文本的质量差异巨大却被忽视。\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Binary detectors (DivEye, Min-K%++) 不区分代际；model collapse 文献 (Shumailov et al., Alemohammad et al. 2024, Dohmatob et al. 2024) 描述宏观现象但不提供检测工具；data selection methods (DSIR, D4, DoReMi) 做分布匹配但不估计递归深度；Drayson et al. (2025) 将 binary MGT detection 应用于 importance resampling 以防止 model collapse，是目前最接近的工作。然而这些方法均做二值判断，**不估计文本经历了多少轮递归生成，也不量化辨别能力的饱和边界。**\\n- **Our framing**: 将递归生成建模为 iterated lossy channel，从信息论（DPI）推导出 K* 的必然存在。K* 是首次系统测量的 operational quantity——它告诉数据策展者\\\"在什么深度以下，depth-aware curation 比 binary detection 更有价值\\\"。通过 8 条件实验矩阵（4 model-mode configurations + 4 decoding strategies）系统表征 K*，揭示其 universality：**K*=2 在所有测试条件下不变**，表明递归生成存在一个与具体模型架构和解码策略无关的 universal discrimination boundary。这一发现为数据治理提供 actionable guidance——depth-aware filtering 在 K* 以内有效，且无需针对不同模型或解码配置分别调优。\\n- **Headline result template**: \\\"Recursive generation has a universal discrimination boundary at K*=2: invariant across 4 model-mode configurations and 4 decoding strategies (8 conditions total) at ≤1.5B scale, with cross-model transfer retention of 80-92% confirming depth features are largely text-intrinsic. Even this coarse 3-class signal yields X% improvement over binary filtering on MMLU/HellaSwag.\\\"\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Responses\\n\\n1. **\\\"Universality claim 只基于 ≤1.5B 模型，能推广到更大模型吗？\\\"** → 这是最大 limitation，论文明确标注 \\\"at ≤1.5B scale\\\"；但 (i) universality 在 4 个不同架构族（GPT-2 style, Pythia, OLMo, Qwen）间已成立，说明不是单一架构的 artifact；(ii) DPI 理论保证 K*_theory 对任意规模模型存在，实证 K*_empirical 在更大模型上可能更大（信号更丰富）也可能不变（lossy channel 特性稳定），这是重要的 future work\\n2. **\\\"只是 feature engineering + off-the-shelf classifier，方法贡献弱\\\"** → 贡献在 problem formulation + empirical characterization（首次测量 K*）+ 理论框架（DPI）+ universality 发现，不是分类器本身\\n3. **\\\"Prompt-chain 不代表真实世界递归污染\\\"** → retrain-chain ecological validity check 作为补充；prompt-chain 是 controlled experiment 的标准方法\\n4. **\\\"K*=2 太浅，graduated filtering 收益有限\\\"** → universality 恰好说明这是 fundamental boundary 而非实验 artifact；即使 K*=2，3-class（human/gen-1/gen-2+）比 binary 在阈值附近更 calibrated（exp-006）；K* 的 universality 意味着实践者无需为每个模型/解码配置单独标定阈值\\n5. **\\\"同模型做生成和 surprisal 计算有 bias\\\"** → **exp-013 直接反驳**：Pythia↔OLMo 90-92% accuracy retention，0v1 transfer AUC > 0.96。Depth signal 主要编码在文本而非 model self-familiarity\\n6. **\\\"为什么不用 learned representations\\\"** → **exp-015 直接回应**：frozen LLM embeddings + MLP probe 达到 K*=3（vs 15D K*=2），揭示 representation-dependent hierarchy: K*_theory ≥ K*_neural ≥ K*_15D。Hand-crafted 15D features 的优势在于 model-free transferability（80-92% cross-model retention）、可解释性（支持 degradation ordering 分析）、实用可部署性。Neural representations 多辨别 1 个深度级别但代价是 model dependence + ~100x 计算开销。这一 tradeoff 证明 15D features 是 practical choice，同时表明 theoretical boundary 延伸至 K*=2 之上\\n7. **\\\"Graduated filtering 没和 data selection methods 比\\\"** → exp-006 包含 DSIR baseline + α sensitivity sweep\\n8. **\\\"Decoding strategy 只测了 4 种，coverage 不够\\\"** → 覆盖了实践中最常见的 decoding 配置（greedy, nucleus sampling, temperature scaling high/low）；4/4 完全一致强化 universality claim；extreme decoding（top-k=1 之外的 pathological 配置）留作 future work\\n9. **\\\"为什么不直接用 LLM 做 depth estimation\\\"** → **exp-011 直接反驳**：GPT-5.5 和 Claude Opus 4.6 在 500 样本 zero-shot 5-class depth 分类上仅 33.1-33.2% accuracy（MAE ~1.1），低于 OBD 15-feature LightGBM 的 38.6%。二者 binary detection 均 ~88%，说明 LLM 能区分 human vs synthetic 但无法估计递归深度。depth estimation 是非平凡任务，需要专用特征工程\\n\\n## Venue Fit\\n\\n- EMNLP 2026 special theme 明确呼吁 data contamination, synthetic data feedback loops, data governance 方向的工作\\n- 2+1 claim 结构：C1' 是 comprehensive characterization study with universality finding，C3 是 practical application，generation mode analysis 增加深度\\n- Universality narrative 比 controllability 更有 impact：从 \\\"K* depends on X, Y, Z\\\" 升级为 \\\"K*=2 is a universal boundary\\\"，审稿人更容易记住和传播\\n- 风险：universality claim 在 ≤1.5B 规模下的外推性是最大攻击面 → 论文中 scope 明确、limitation 诚实、DPI 理论支撑泛化的合理性\\n\", \"total_chars\": 10447, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

(instruction) | N/A | 61-63% retention | **upper bound with mode-specific confound** — 仅作 ablation |\\\\n\\\\n**原则**：\\\\n- C1' 的跨模型 claim 必须基于 continuation mode 数据\\\\n- Rewrite mode 的高 AUC (0.6957 vs cont 0.6114) 是 mode-specific signal amplification，不能作为泛化性证据\\\\n- mvp-001 (Qwen-rewrite) 定位为 \\\\\\\"pilot study demonstrating feasibility\\\\\\\"，后续用 continuation mode rigorously validate\\\\n- cont→rw 89% vs rw→cont 82% 不对称性 → continuation features 是 rewrite 的通用子集\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims (2+1 结构, D012)\\\\n\\\\n### Primary: C1' (K* Characterization)\\\\n首次系统测量递归合成文本的 generational discrimination boundary K*，并揭示其 universality。\\\\n\\\\nDPI 理论证明有限 K*_theory 必然存在（信息论 impossibility boundary）；我们首次在受控实验中测量 K*_empirical，它提供 K*_theory 的保守下界。通过 8 条件实验矩阵（4 model-mode configurations + 4 decoding strategies）系统表征 K*_empirical 的行为，发现 K*=2 在所有 8 个测试条件下保持不变，展现出跨模型架构、跨解码策略、跨生成模式的 universality。\\\\n\\\\n**子组件：**\\\\n\\\\n**(a) K*=2 universality across architectures, generation modes, and decoding strategies**\\\\n> **4/4 model-mode configurations 与 4/4 decoding strategies 全部 K*=2：**\\\\n>\\\\n> *Model × mode (exp-007):*\\\\n> | Model | AUC 1v2 | 95% CI | CI lower > 0.60? |\\\\n> |-------|---------|--------|-----------------|\\\\n> | Qwen-rewrite | 0.6955 | [0.6859, 0.7041] | ✅ (+8.6pp) |\\\\n> | Pythia-1.4B | 0.6539 | [0.6454, 0.6630] | ✅ (+4.5pp) |\\\\n> | OLMo-1B | 0.6145 | [0.6045, 0.6247] | ✅ (+0.45pp) |\\\\n> | Qwen-cont | 0.6112 | [0.6015, 0.6213] | ✅ (+0.15pp, 最边缘) |\\\\n>\\\\n> *Decoding strategy invariance (exp-003):*\\\\n> | Decoding | AUC 1v2 | K* |\\\\n> |----------|---------|-----|\\\\n> | greedy | 0.6893 | 2 |\\\\n> | nucleus p=0.9 | 0.6866 | 2 |\\\\n> | temp=0.7 | 0.6887 | 2 |\\\\n> | temp=1.2 | 0.7039 | 2 |\\\\n>\\\\n> - 全部 1v2 CI lower > 0.60, 全部 2v3 CI upper < 0.60, 全部 permutation p < 0.002\\\\n> - 4 decoding strategies 的 AUC 1v2 range [0.6866, 0.7039]，K* 完全不变\\\\n> - K*=2 在所有 8 个测试条件下保持不变（4 model-mode configurations + 4 decoding strategies）\\\\n> - ⚠️ θ-sensitivity: θ=0.60 下 4/4 K*=2；θ=0.70 下仅 Config4-T12 K*=2（1v2=0.7039 仅超 0.70 阈值 0.004），其余 3 config K*=1。论文以 θ=0.60 为主结论，θ=0.70 边界效应作 footnote\\\\n> - ⚠️ 特征排序分化: K* 跨 config 稳定，但特征重要性排序不完全一致——Greedy vs T1.2 Spearman=0.53, p=0.041（Bonferroni 校正后阈值 0.0083，不显著）。论文中措辞应为 \\\\\\\"suggestive\\\\\\\" 而非确定性结论。Config4-T12 正确 top-3 features: hapax_ratio, ttr, surp_skew（非 freq_entropy）\\\\n> - ⚠️ K*=2 边缘性双端注意: Qwen-cont 1v2 CI lower=0.6015（+0.15pp 高于 θ）+ Qwen-rw 2v3 CI upper=0.5991（-0.09pp 低于 θ），K*=2 边界在两端都极边缘。Discussion/Limitations 需同时提及\\\\n> - Paper 可写：\\\\\\\"K*=2 is established as a consistent discrimination boundary across all 8 tested conditions (4 model-mode configurations and 4 decoding strategies; bootstrap 95% CI for AUC₁ᵥ₂ > 0.60; AUC₂ᵥ₃ CI upper < 0.60), suggesting universality beyond the ≤1.5B scale studied.\\\\\\\"\\\\n> - ⚠️ 措辞策略（D017 Reviewer 评估）：分两层——\\\\\\\"established as consistent\\\\\\\" 用确定性语言（8/8 有统计检验支撑），\\\\\\\"suggesting universality beyond\\\\\\\" 对外推 hedging。不升级为 \\\\\\\"establishing universal\\\\\\\"——3 模型 ≤1.5B 会引发审稿人质疑消耗 rebuttal 预算\\\\n> - ⚠️ C1' 子组件定位：呈现为\\\\\\\"对同一 claim 的 4 个 evidence 维度\\\\\\\"而非 4 个独立 finding。§4 标题用 \\\\\\\"K* Characterization\\\\\\\"，每个子节开头显式回答一个 reviewer concern，子节间用 transitional sentences 链接\\\\n\\\\n**(b) Cross-model transfer**\\\\n> - Pythia↔OLMo 90-92% accuracy retention → K*=2 不是 model self-familiarity artifact\\\\n> - 0v1 AUC > 0.96 across all transfer pairs → binary contamination signal 完全跨模型可迁移\\\\n> - 1v2+ transfer 取决于模型族相似度 → 与 K*=2 完美一致（K* 之上分辨力本就微弱）\\\\n> - transfer retention 80-92% suggests depth signal has a large text-intrinsic component（不量化具体比例，因 transfer accuracy retention ≠ 信号分解）\\\\n> - ⚠️ 数值精度：Pythia→OLMo 91.6%, OLMo→Pythia 90.3%，范围 90-92%（非 91-92%）；Qwen-cont↔Pythia 84.7%/85.2%，范围 84-85%（非 83-85%）\\\\n\\\\n**(c) Degradation ordering consistency**\\\\n> - Spearman ρ ≥ 0.85 on single-feature AUC ranking（mode-controlled, continuation）\\\\n> - Top discriminative features shared: surp_d1_std, surp_std, surp_d2_std\\\\n> - Limitation: depth-pair level 一致性在 1v2 处较弱（τ~0.37）\\\\n> - Metric: single-feature AUC ranking（model-free, not LightGBM split count）\\\\n\\\\n**(e) Representation dependence of K* (§4.x Neural Probe / §5.x Discussion)**\\\\n> - exp-015: frozen Qwen-1.5B embeddings (1536-dim) + MLP probe → K*(neural)=3 on Qwen-cont（2v3 AUC=0.6195, fold range [0.6052, 0.6323]）\\\\n> - K* hierarchy: K*_theory ≥ K*_neural ≥ K*_15D，证明 K* 是表示复杂度的函数，K*_15D=2 是操作下界而非信息论极限\\\\n> - Interpretability–discriminability tradeoff: 15D features（K*=2, model-free, interpretable, deployable）vs neural probe（K*=3, model-dependent, opaque, 100x+ compute）\\\\n> - ⚠️ 当前仅 Qwen-cont 1 个数据集（Pythia/OLMo 待重跑）。若 Pythia/OLMo K*_neural=2 → self-familiarity 效应（Qwen embeddings 对 Qwen 生成文本更敏感），反而加强 15D model-free features 的定位\\\\n> - ⚠️ 2v3 边界极度边缘（fold 2 仅 +0.52pp 超 θ），需 bootstrap CI + permutation test 统计确认（重跑脚本已含）\\\\n> - 论文定位：**深化 K* characterization**（不推翻 K*=2 headline）。Headline 保持 \\\\\\\"K*_empirical=2 under 15D hand-crafted features\\\\\\\"，新增子结论 \\\\\\\"K*_neural=3 demonstrates representation-dependent boundary\\\\\\\"\\\\n> - Discussion 结构：§5.1 现有内容保留 + §5.1bis \\\\\\\"Representation Dependence of K*\\\\\\\" 独立子节（积极发现，非 limitation）\\\\n> - 预防审稿人 \\\\\\\"why bury the stronger result?\\\\\\\"：K*_15D=2 是 practical/deployable boundary，K*_neural=3 是 theoretical exploration，两者服务不同目的\\\\n\\\\n**(d) Generation mode analysis (§4.x Analysis subsection)**\\\\n> - Signal strength: rewrite AUC 1v2=0.6957 vs continuation 0.6114（Δ=+8.4pp）\\\\n> - Transferability: continuation 80-92% retention vs rewrite 61-63%\\\\n> - Feature subset: cont→rw 89% > rw→cont 82% → continuation features 是 rewrite 的通用子集\\\\n> - Interpretation: rewrite mode 的 instruction-following 引入 mode-specific style artifacts，放大 depth signal 但降低跨模型泛化性\\\\n> - 论文定位：Analysis subsection（\\\\\\\"How does generation mode affect depth estimation?\\\\\\\"），不是独立 claim\\\\n\\\\n### Secondary: C3 (Graduated Filtering)\\\\n即使在 K*=2 的粗粒度 ordinal signal（human/gen-1/gen-2+ 三级）下，K*-aware graduated filtering 仍优于 binary filtering，因为 ordinal signal 在阈值附近提供更 calibrated 的质量估计。\\\\n\\\\n### Supporting Analysis: Canonical Condition Selection\\\\nGeneration mode 是 depth estimation 的显著混淆变量。Methodology 段落阐述 continuation mode 作为 canonical condition 的选择及理由（不作为独立 claim）。\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: Web 数据正被多代递归合成文本污染。当前检测方法将合成文本视为 binary 类别（real vs synthetic），但质量随代际深度非线性退化——首代合成文本与四代合成文本的质量差异巨大却被忽视。\\\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Binary detectors (DivEye, Min-K%++) 不区分代际；model collapse 文献 (Shumailov et al., Alemohammad et al. 2024, Dohmatob et al. 2024) 描述宏观现象但不提供检测工具；data selection methods (DSIR, D4, DoReMi) 做分布匹配但不估计递归深度；Drayson et al. (2025) 将 binary MGT detection 应用于 importance resampling 以防止 model collapse，是目前最接近的工作。然而这些方法均做二值判断，**不估计文本经历了多少轮递归生成，也不量化辨别能力的饱和边界。**\\\\n- **Our framing**: 将递归生成建模为 iterated lossy channel，从信息论（DPI）推导出 K* 的必然存在。K* 是首次系统测量的 operational quantity——它告诉数据策展者\\\\\\\"在什么深度以下，depth-aware curation 比 binary detection 更有价值\\\\\\\"。通过 8 条件实验矩阵（4 model-mode configurations + 4 decoding strategies）系统表征 K*，揭示其 universality：**K*=2 在所有测试条件下不变**，表明递归生成存在一个与具体模型架构和解码策略无关的 universal discrimination boundary。这一发现为数据治理提供 actionable guidance——depth-aware filtering 在 K* 以内有效，且无需针对不同模型或解码配置分别调优。\\\\n- **Headline result template**: \\\\\\\"Recursive generation has a universal discrimination boundary at K*=2: invariant across 4 model-mode configurations and 4 decoding strategies (8 conditions total) at ≤1.5B scale, with cross-model transfer retention of 80-92% confirming depth features are largely text-intrinsic. Even this coarse 3-class signal yields X% improvement over binary filtering on MMLU/HellaSwag.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Responses\\\\n\\\\n1. **\\\\\\\"Universality claim 只基于 ≤1.5B 模型，能推广到更大模型吗？\\\\\\\"** → 这是最大 limitation，论文明确标注 \\\\\\\"at ≤1.5B scale\\\\\\\"；但 (i) universality 在 4 个不同架构族（GPT-2 style, Pythia, OLMo, Qwen）间已成立，说明不是单一架构的 artifact；(ii) DPI 理论保证 K*_theory 对任意规模模型存在，实证 K*_empirical 在更大模型上可能更大（信号更丰富）也可能不变（lossy channel 特性稳定），这是重要的 future work\\\\n2. **\\\\\\\"只是 feature engineering + off-the-shelf classifier，方法贡献弱\\\\\\\"** → 贡献在 problem formulation + empirical characterization（首次测量 K*）+ 理论框架（DPI）+ universality 发现，不是分类器本身\\\\n3. **\\\\\\\"Prompt-chain 不代表真实世界递归污染\\\\\\\"** → retrain-chain ecological validity check 作为补充；prompt-chain 是 controlled experiment 的标准方法\\\\n4. **\\\\\\\"K*=2 太浅，graduated filtering 收益有限\\\\\\\"** → universality 恰好说明这是 fundamental boundary 而非实验 artifact；即使 K*=2，3-class（human/gen-1/gen-2+）比 binary 在阈值附近更 calibrated（exp-006）；K* 的 universality 意味着实践者无需为每个模型/解码配置单独标定阈值\\\\n5. **\\\\\\\"同模型做生成和 surprisal 计算有 bias\\\\\\\"** → **exp-013 直接反驳**：Pythia↔OLMo 90-92% accuracy retention，0v1 transfer AUC > 0.96。Depth signal 主要编码在文本而非 model self-familiarity\\\\n6. **\\\\\\\"为什么不用 learned representations\\\\\\\"** → **exp-015 直接回应**：frozen LLM embeddings + MLP probe 达到 K*=3（vs 15D K*=2），揭示 representation-dependent hierarchy: K*_theory ≥ K*_neural ≥ K*_15D。Hand-crafted 15D features 的优势在于 model-free transferability（80-92% cross-model retention）、可解释性（支持 degradation ordering 分析）、实用可部署性。Neural representations 多辨别 1 个深度级别但代价是 model dependence + ~100x 计算开销。这一 tradeoff 证明 15D features 是 practical choice，同时表明 theoretical boundary 延伸至 K*=2 之上\\\\n7. **\\\\\\\"Graduated filtering 没和 data selection methods 比\\\\\\\"** → exp-006 包含 DSIR baseline + α sensitivity sweep\\\\n8. **\\\\\\\"Decoding strategy 只测了 4 种，coverage 不够\\\\\\\"** → 覆盖了实践中最常见的 decoding 配置（greedy, nucleus sampling, temperature scaling high/low）；4/4 完全一致强化 universality claim；extreme decoding（top-k=1 之外的 pathological 配置）留作 future work\\\\n9. **\\\\\\\"为什么不直接用 LLM 做 depth estimation\\\\\\\"** → **exp-011 直接反驳**：GPT-5.5 和 Claude Opus 4.6 在 500 样本 zero-shot 5-class depth 分类上仅 33.1-33.2% accuracy（MAE ~1.1），低于 OBD 15-feature LightGBM 的 38.6%。二者 binary detection 均 ~88%，说明 LLM 能区分 human vs synthetic 但无法估计递归深度。depth estimation 是非平凡任务，需要专用特征工程\\\\n\\\\n## Venue Fit\\\\n\\\\n- EMNLP 2026 special theme 明确呼吁 data contamination, synthetic data feedback loops, data governance 方向的工作\\\\n- 2+1 claim 结构：C1' 是 comprehensive characterization study with universality finding，C3 是 practical application，generation mode analysis 增加深度\\\\n- Universality narrative 比 controllability 更有 impact：从 \\\\\\\"K* depends on X, Y, Z\\\\\\\" 升级为 \\\\\\\"K*=2 is a universal boundary\\\\\\\"，审稿人更容易记住和传播\\\\n- 风险：universality claim 在 ≤1.5B 规模下的外推性是最大攻击面 → 论文中 scope 明确、limitation 诚实、DPI 理论支撑泛化的合理性\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 10447, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

0.6114（Δ=+8.4pp）\\n> - Transferability: continuation 80-92% retention vs rewrite 61-63%\\n> - Feature subset: cont→rw 89% > rw→cont 82% → continuation features 是 rewrite 的通用子集\\n> - Interpretation: rewrite mode 的 instruction-following 引入 mode-specific style artifacts，放大 depth signal 但降低跨模型泛化性\\n> - 论文定位：Analysis subsection（\\\"How does generation mode affect depth estimation?\\\"），不是独立 claim\\n\\n### Secondary: C3 (Graduated Filtering)\\n即使在 K*=2 的粗粒度 ordinal signal（human/gen-1/gen-2+ 三级）下，K*-aware graduated filtering 仍优于 binary filtering，因为 ordinal signal 在阈值附近提供更 calibrated 的质量估计。\\n\\n### Supporting Analysis: Canonical Condition Selection\\nGeneration mode 是 depth estimation 的显著混淆变量。Methodology 段落阐述 continuation mode 作为 canonical condition 的选择及理由（不作为独立 claim）。\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: Web 数据正被多代递归合成文本污染。当前检测方法将合成文本视为 binary 类别（real vs synthetic），但质量随代际深度非线性退化——首代合成文本与四代合成文本的质量差异巨大却被忽视。\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Binary detectors (DivEye, Min-K%++) 不区分代际；model collapse 文献 (Shumailov et al., Alemohammad et al. 2024, Dohmatob et al. 2024) 描述宏观现象但不提供检测工具；data selection methods (DSIR, D4, DoReMi) 做分布匹配但不估计递归深度；Drayson et al. (2025) 将 binary MGT detection 应用于 importance resampling 以防止 model collapse，是目前最接近的工作。然而这些方法均做二值判断，**不估计文本经历了多少轮递归生成，也不量化辨别能力的饱和边界。**\\n- **Our framing**: 将递归生成建模为 iterated lossy channel，从信息论（DPI）推导出 K* 的必然存在。K* 是首次系统测量的 operational quantity——它告诉数据策展者\\\"在什么深度以下，depth-aware curation 比 binary detection 更有价值\\\"。通过 8 条件实验矩阵（4 model-mode configurations + 4 decoding strategies）系统表征 K*，揭示其 universality：**K*=2 在所有测试条件下不变**，表明递归生成存在一个与具体模型架构和解码策略无关的 universal discrimination boundary。这一发现为数据治理提供 actionable guidance——depth-aware filtering 在 K* 以内有效，且无需针对不同模型或解码配置分别调优。\\n- **Headline result template**: \\\"Recursive generation has a universal discrimination boundary at K*=2: invariant across 4 model-mode configurations and 4 decoding strategies (8 conditions total) at ≤1.5B scale, with cross-model transfer retention of 80-92% confirming depth features are largely text-intrinsic. Even this coarse 3-class signal yields X% improvement over binary filtering on MMLU/HellaSwag.\\\"\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & Responses\\n\\n1. **\\\"Universality claim 只基于 ≤1.5B 模型，能推广到更大模型吗？\\\"** → 这是最大 limitation，论文明确标注 \\\"at ≤1.5B scale\\\"；但 (i) universality 在 4 个不同架构族（GPT-2 style, Pythia, OLMo, Qwen）间已成立，说明不是单一架构的 artifact；(ii) DPI 理论保证 K*_theory 对任意规模模型存在，实证 K*_empirical 在更大模型上可能更大（信号更丰富）也可能不变（lossy channel 特性稳定），这是重要的 future work\\n2. **\\\"只是 feature engineering + off-the-shelf classifier，方法贡献弱\\\"** → 贡献在 problem formulation + empirical characterization（首次测量 K*）+ 理论框架（DPI）+ universality 发现，不是分类器本身\\n3. **\\\"Prompt-chain 不代表真实世界递归污染\\\"** → retrain-chain ecological validity check 作为补充；prompt-chain 是 controlled experiment 的标准方法\\n4. **\\\"K*=2 太浅，graduated filtering 收益有限\\\"** → universality 恰好说明这是 fundamental boundary 而非实验 artifact；即使 K*=2，3-class（human/gen-1/gen-2+）比 binary 在阈值附近更 calibrated（exp-006）；K* 的 universality 意味着实践者无需为每个模型/解码配置单独标定阈值\\n5. **\\\"同模型做生成和 surprisal 计算有 bias\\\"** → **exp-013 直接反驳**：Pythia↔OLMo 90-92% accuracy retention，0v1 transfer AUC > 0.96。Depth signal 主要编码在文本而非 model self-familiarity\\n6. **\\\"为什么不用 learned representations\\\"** → **exp-015 直接回应**：frozen LLM embeddings + MLP probe 达到 K*=3（vs 15D K*=2），揭示 representation-dependent hierarchy: K*_theory ≥ K*_neural ≥ K*_15D。Hand-crafted 15D features 的优势在于 model-free transferability（80-92% cross-model retention）、可解释性（支持 degradation ordering 分析）、实用可部署性。Neural representations 多辨别 1 个深度级别但代价是 model dependence + ~100x 计算开销。这一 tradeoff 证明 15D features 是 practical choice，同时表明 theoretical boundary 延伸至 K*=2 之上\\n7. **\\\"Graduated filtering 没和 data selection methods 比\\\"** → exp-006 包含 DSIR baseline + α sensitivity sweep\\n8. **\\\"Decoding strategy 只测了 4 种，coverage 不够\\\"** → 覆盖了实践中最常见的 decoding 配置（greedy, nucleus sampling, temperature scaling high/low）；4/4 完全一致强化 universality claim；extreme decoding（top-k=1 之外的 pathological 配置）留作 future work\\n9. **\\\"为什么不直接用 LLM 做 depth estimation\\\"** → **exp-011 直接反驳**：GPT-5.5 和 Claude Opus 4.6 在 500 样本 zero-shot 5-class depth 分类上仅 33.1-33.2% accuracy（MAE ~1.1），低于 OBD 15-feature LightGBM 的 38.6%。二者 binary detection 均 ~88%，说明 LLM 能区分 human vs synthetic 但无法估计递归深度。depth estimation 是非平凡任务，需要专用特征工程\\n\\n## Venue Fit\\n\\n- EMNLP 2026 special theme 明确呼吁 data contamination, synthetic data feedback loops, data governance 方向的工作\\n- 2+1 claim 结构：C1' 是 comprehensive characterization study with universality finding，C3 是 practical application，generation mode analysis 增加深度\\n- Universality narrative 比 controllability 更有 impact：从 \\\"K* depends on X, Y, Z\\\" 升级为 \\\"K*=2 is a universal boundary\\\"，审稿人更容易记住和传播\\n- 风险：universality claim 在 ≤1.5B 规模下的外推性是最大攻击面 → 论文中 scope 明确、limitation 诚实、DPI 理论支撑泛化的合理性\\n\", \"total_chars\": 10447, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"
    }

```text
1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We evaluate generational depth estimation on four model configurations:
5	Pythia-1.4B and OLMo-1B in continuation mode, and Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct in both rewrite and continuation modes.
6	All experiments use the OBD-LightGBM classifier with the 15-dimensional feature set described in Section~\ref{sec:method}.
7	Each configuration uses a balanced corpus of 5{,}000 texts per depth level (generations 0--5).
8	We organize results around four questions:
9	(1)~Does $\Kempirical = 2$ hold across configurations?
10	(2)~Do depth classifiers transfer across models?
11	(3)~Is the feature degradation ordering model-independent?
12	(4)~How does generation mode affect detectability?
13	
14	%% ====================================================================
15	\subsection{$\Kempirical = 2$ Across All Configurations}
16	\label{sec:kstar_boundary}
17	
18	Table~\ref{tab:kstar_ci} reports pairwise $\auc$ for adjacent depth pairs
19	across all four configurations, with bootstrap 95\% confidence intervals
20	(1{,}000 resamples) and permutation-test $p$-values for the critical 1v2
21	and 2v3 boundaries.
22	
23	\begin{table}[t]
24	\centering
25	\small
26	\caption{Pairwise $\auc$ for adjacent depth pairs.  Bootstrap 95\% CIs
27	and permutation $p$-values shown for the $\Kstar$ boundary rows (1v2,
28	2v3).  $\Kempirical = 2$ requires 1v2 $\text{CI}_\text{low} > \theta$
29	and 2v3 $\text{CI}_\text{high} < \theta$ with $\theta = 0.60$.
30	All 0v1 permutation $p \leq 0.002$ (Qwen-continuation 0v1: CI [0.9963, 0.9978], $p = 0.002$).}
31	\label{tab:kstar_ci}
32	\begin{tabular}{lcccc}
33	\toprule
34	 & Qwen-rw & Pythia & OLMo & Qwen-cont \\
35	\midrule
36	0v1 & 0.9956 & 0.9932 & 0.9970 & 0.9971 \\[4pt]
37	1v2 & 0.6955 & 0.6539 & 0.6145 & 0.6112 \\
38	{\footnotesize CI}
39	  & {\footnotesize [.6859,\,.7041]}
40	  & {\footnotesize [.6454,\,.6630]}
41	  & {\footnotesize [.6045,\,.6247]}
42	  & {\footnotesize [.6015,\,.6213]} \\
43	{\footnotesize $p$}
44	  & {\footnotesize .002}
45	  & {\footnotesize .002}
46	  & {\footnotesize .002}
47	  & {\footnotesize .002} \\[4pt]
48	2v3 & 0.5893 & 0.5514 & 0.5496 & 0.5609 \\
49	{\footnotesize CI}
50	  & {\footnotesize [.5795,\,.5991]}
51	  & {\footnotesize [.5417,\,.5628]}
52	  & {\footnotesize [.5399,\,.5596]}
53	  & {\footnotesize [.5511,\,.5712]} \\
54	{\footnotesize $p$}
55	  & {\footnotesize .002}
56	  & {\footnotesize .002}
57	  & {\footnotesize .002}
58	  & {\footnotesize .002} \\[4pt]
59	3v4 & 0.5435 & 0.5152 & 0.5091 & 0.5409 \\
60	4v5 & 0.5340 & 0.5136 & 0.5045 & 0.5141 \\
61	\bottomrule
62	\end{tabular}
63	\end{table}
64	
65	The pattern is consistent across all four configurations:
66	0v1 discrimination is near-perfect ($\auc > 0.99$),
67	followed by a sharp drop at 1v2 ($\auc$ range: 0.6112--0.6955),
68	with 2v3 falling below the decision threshold $\theta = 0.60$
69	($\auc$ range: 0.5496--0.5893).
70	All subsequent pairs (3v4, 4v5) converge toward chance.
71	Figure~\ref{fig:auc_decay} visualizes this decay.
72	
73	\begin{figure}[t]
74	\centering
75	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig2_auc_decay.pdf}
76	\caption{Pairwise $\auc$ as a function of depth pair across four model-mode configurations (solid) and four decoding strategies (dashed).  All configurations show a sharp drop between 0v1 and 1v2, with 2v3 and deeper pairs falling below the decision threshold $\theta = 0.60$ (horizontal dashed line).  Error bars show bootstrap 95\% CIs for model-mode configurations; decoding strategy curves omit CIs as they were computed on a separate experimental setup.}
77	\label{fig:auc_decay}
78	\end{figure}
79	
80	For the $\Kempirical$ determination, all four 1v2 CI lower bounds exceed
81	$\theta$: Qwen-rewrite ($\text{CI}_\text{low} = 0.6859$), Pythia (0.6454),
82	OLMo (0.6045), and Qwen-continuation (0.6015).
83	Conversely, all four 2v3 CI upper bounds fall below $\theta$:
84	Qwen-rewrite ($\text{CI}_\text{high} = 0.5991$), Qwen-continuation (0.5712),
85	Pythia (0.5628), and OLMo (0.5596).
86	Permutation tests confirm $p = 0.002$ for all eight boundary comparisons.
87	Together these establish $\Kempirical = 2$ as a consistent discrimination boundary across all tested configurations at the $\leq$1.5B scale.
88	
89	The most marginal case is Qwen-continuation, where the 1v2 CI lower bound
90	(0.6015) exceeds $\theta$ by only 0.0015.  This narrow margin
91	indicates that $\Kempirical = 2$ is robust but not trivially so---continuation-mode
92	generation produces weaker depth artifacts than rewrite mode, pushing the
93	boundary closer to the detection threshold.
94	
95	The absolute 1v2 $\auc$ varies across models:
96	Qwen-rewrite (0.6955) $>$ Pythia (0.6539) $>$ OLMo (0.6145) $\approx$ Qwen-continuation (0.6112).
97	Notably, the spread due to generation mode within a single model
98	(Qwen-rewrite vs.\ Qwen-continuation: $\Delta = 8.4$\,pp) exceeds the spread
99	across models in the same mode
100	(Pythia vs.\ OLMo: $\Delta = 3.9$\,pp),
101	suggesting that generation protocol is a stronger determinant
102	of detectability than model identity.
103	We examine both factors in Sections~\ref{sec:transfer}
104	and~\ref{sec:generation_mode}.
105	
106	Beyond the $\Kempirical$ boundary, depth discrimination deteriorates
107	rapidly.
108	OLMo shows nonsignificant permutation $p$-values at 3v4
109	($p = 0.064$) and 4v5 ($p = 0.232$), indicating that depth
110	classification beyond generation~2 is indistinguishable from
111	random for this model.
112	Pythia retains marginal effects at these depths
113	($p = 0.006$ and $p = 0.008$, respectively),
114	while Qwen-continuation shows weak but detectable discrimination through
115	3v4 ($p = 0.002$) that fades at 4v5 ($p = 0.006$).
116	
117	\paragraph{Classifier design justification.}
118	The OBD formulation is motivated by a three-way comparison with multiclass LightGBM and ordinal logistic regression (Section~\ref{sec:method}).
119	Across all three model families, OBD achieves the best accuracy (38.2--42.3\%, i.e., $+$21.5--25.6\,pp over the 16.7\% random baseline for 6-class classification, and $+$5.2--9.3\,pp over LLM zero-shot at ${\sim}$33\%; cf.\ Table~\ref{tab:s3_llm_zeroshot}) while maintaining competitive MAE.
120	Ordinal logistic regression yields the lowest MAE (e.g., 0.911 vs.\ 0.984 OBD on Qwen-rewrite) but at the cost of near-chance pairwise AUC (mean 0.49 vs.\ 0.61 for OBD across families)---it reduces error by collapsing predictions toward central depths rather than genuinely distinguishing adjacent levels.
121	Multiclass LightGBM matches OBD on pairwise AUC but consistently trails in both accuracy and MAE.
122	OBD thus provides the best balance across all three evaluation axes, justifying its use as the default classifier throughout.
123	
124	%% ====================================================================
125	\subsection{Cross-Model Transfer}
126	\label{sec:transfer}
127	
128	Having established $\Kempirical = 2$ consistently, we next ask: is this boundary a property of the text itself, or an artifact of model self-familiarity?
129	To answer this, we train classifiers on one model's outputs and evaluate on another's.
130	Table~\ref{tab:transfer_matrix} shows the full
131	$4 \times 4$ transfer accuracy matrix for 6-class ordinal depth
132	classification.
133	
134	\begin{table}[t]
135	\centering
136	\small
137	\caption{Cross-model transfer accuracy (6-class ordinal
138	classification).  Diagonal entries (bold): within-model accuracy.
139	Off-diagonal: cross-model transfer.  Retention is computed as
140	off-diagonal accuracy divided by the diagonal entry of the training
141	model.}
142	\label{tab:transfer_matrix}
143	\begin{tabular}{lcccc}
144	\toprule
145	Train $\downarrow$ / Test $\rightarrow$
146	  & Qwen-rw & Pythia & OLMo & Qwen-cont \\
147	\midrule
148	Qwen-rw
149	  & \textbf{0.4229} & 0.2590 & 0.2653 & 0.3466 \\
150	Pythia
151	  & 0.2718 & \textbf{0.3856} & 0.3531 & 0.3287 \\
152	OLMo
153	  & 0.2768 & 0.3450 & \textbf{0.3822} & 0.3244 \\
154	Qwen-cont
155	  & 0.3440 & 0.3284 & 0.3112 & \textbf{0.3875} \\
156	\bottomrule
157	\end{tabular}
158	\end{table}
159	
160	\begin{figure}[t]
161	\centering
162	\includegraphics[width=0.85\columnwidth]{figures/fig3_transfer_heatmap.pdf}
163	\caption{Cross-model transfer heatmap (6-class ordinal accuracy).
164	Rows: training model; columns: test model.
165	Pythia$\leftrightarrow$OLMo (both continuation mode) shows high
166	mutual transfer (90--92\% accuracy retention), while Qwen-rewrite
167	transfers poorly to continuation-mode models (61--63\%).
168	Diagonal entries are within-model baselines.}
169	\label{fig:transfer_heatmap}
170	\end{figure}
171	
172	Two principal findings emerge (Figure~\ref{fig:transfer_heatmap}).
173	First, transfer between continuation-mode models is high.
174	Pythia$\leftrightarrow$OLMo mutual transfer retains 90--92\% of within-model accuracy
175	(85--86\% retention by Kendall $\tau$).
176	The accuracy-based retention of 90--92\% is complemented by the
177	$\tau$-based retention of 85--86\%, indicating that not only
178	classification accuracy but also the relative depth ordering of
179	predictions is largely preserved.
180	Qwen-continuation$\leftrightarrow$Pythia also transfers well
181	(84--85\% accuracy retention),
182	and Qwen-continuation$\leftrightarrow$OLMo achieves 80--85\%.
183	These retention rates---80--92\% of within-model accuracy preserved across continuation-mode pairs---suggest that a large component of the depth signal is text-intrinsic rather than tied to a specific generator.
184	
185	Second, Qwen-rewrite exhibits catastrophic transfer failure.
186	Classifiers trained on Qwen-rewrite retain only 61--63\% of
187	within-model accuracy when evaluated on Pythia or OLMo
188	(45--50\% by $\tau$).
189	The reverse direction transfers asymmetrically: Pythia/OLMo${\to}$Qwen-rewrite retains 70--72\% of within-model accuracy, higher than the forward 61--63\% but still far below continuation-mode transfer rates (80--92\%).
190	This confirms that rewrite-mode classifiers rely on generation
191	artifacts that do not generalize across models, in contrast
192	to Qwen-continuation, which achieves 80--85\% retention to the same targets.
193	
194	At the 0v1 boundary, cross-model pairwise $\auc$ exceeds 0.96
195	for all model pairs, confirming that the human-vs.-synthetic
196	distinction is consistently transferable across all tested generators
197	and modes.
198	The transfer degradation concentrates at deeper boundaries
199	(1v2 and beyond), where model- and mode-specific factors dominate.
200	
201	The aggregate picture is one of modest cross-model loss:
202	mean pairwise $\auc$ is 0.6099 within-model versus 0.5905
203	cross-model.
204	This gap is driven primarily by Qwen-rewrite; among
205	continuation-mode pairs the within/cross difference narrows
206	further.
207	
208	%% ====================================================================
209	\subsection{Feature Degradation Ordering}
210	\label{sec:feature_degradation}
211	
212	The transfer results indicate that depth signals are largely text-intrinsic.
213	We next ask what drives discrimination: if the signal is text-intrinsic, different generators should rely on the same features in the same order.
214	We test this by computing single-feature $\auc$ rankings
215	independently for each model and comparing them via rank
216	correlation.
217	
218	\begin{table}[t]
219	\centering
220	\small
221	\caption{Top-3 features by single-feature $\auc$ ranking for each
222	model configuration.
223	Features shared across all three continuation-mode models are in bold.
224	Qwen-rewrite's top feature (\texttt{freq\_entropy}) is mode-specific.
225	Spearman $\rho$ (continuation-mode pairs): Pythia--OLMo 0.95,
226	Pythia--Qwen-continuation 0.9143, OLMo--Qwen-continuation 0.85.}
227	\label{tab:top_features}
228	\begin{tabular}{cllll}
229	\toprule
230	Rank & Pythia & OLMo & Qwen-rw & Qwen-cont \\
231	\midrule
232	1
233	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_d1\_std}}
234	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_std}}
235	  & \texttt{freq\_entropy}
236	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_d1\_std}} \\
237	2
238	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_d2\_std}}
239	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_d1\_std}}
240	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_std}}
241	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_std}} \\
242	3
243	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_std}}
244	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_d2\_std}}
245	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_d1\_std}}
246	  & \textbf{\texttt{surp\_d2\_std}} \\
247	\bottomrule
248	\end{tabular}
249	\end{table}
250	
251	Among continuation-mode models, single-feature $\auc$ rankings
252	are highly correlated:
253	Pythia vs.\ OLMo $\rho = 0.95$,
254	Pythia vs.\ Qwen-continuation $\rho = 0.9143$,
255	OLMo vs.\ Qwen-continuation $\rho = 0.85$
256	(all $\rho \geq 0.85$).
257	The top-3 features---\texttt{surp\_d1\_std}, \texttt{surp\_std},
258	and \texttt{surp\_d2\_std}---are shared (up to ordering) across
259	all three continuation-mode configurations
260	(Table~\ref{tab:top_features}).
261	All three measure variability of surprisal or its derivatives,
262	capturing the \emph{heterogeneity} of token-level predictability
263	across the text.
264	That these features degrade in a shared order across generators
265	suggests a common mechanism: each generation step smooths the
266	token-level surprisal landscape, reducing its variance in a
267	model-independent fashion.
268	
269	Qwen-rewrite diverges: its top-ranked feature is
270	\texttt{freq\_entropy}, a lexical diversity measure absent from
271	the continuation-mode top-3 for any model.
272	This corroborates the transfer results in
273	Section~\ref{sec:transfer}---rewrite-mode classifiers learn
274	partly from instruction-following artifacts rather than the
275	shared surprisal-based signals.
276	
277	Kendall $\tau$ provides a complementary, more conservative
278	perspective:
279	Pythia vs.\ OLMo $\tau = 0.733$,
280	Pythia vs.\ Qwen-continuation $\tau = 0.695$,
281	OLMo vs.\ Qwen-continuation $\tau = 0.543$.
282	Cross-mode correlations are lower:
283	Pythia vs.\ Qwen-rewrite $\tau = 0.467$,
284	OLMo vs.\ Qwen-rewrite $\tau = 0.429$.
285	
286	\paragraph{Limitation.}
287	The high Spearman $\rho$ characterizes the \emph{aggregate}
288	feature ranking, but at the depth-pair level the agreement
289	weakens: $\tau \approx 0.37$ at the 1v2 boundary.
290	This means that while the overall importance ordering is shared,
291	the relative contribution of individual features at the
292	discrimination boundary---where precision matters most---varies
293	across models.
294	We view this as a genuine limitation of the text-intrinsic
295	interpretation and a direction for future investigation.
296	
297	%% ====================================================================
298	\subsection{Generation Mode Effect}
299	\label{sec:generation_mode}
300	
```

relative depth ordering of\npredictions is largely preserved.\nQwen-continuation$\\leftrightarrow$Pythia also transfers well\n(84--85\\% accuracy retention),\nand Qwen-continuation$\\leftrightarrow$OLMo achieves 80--85\\%.\nThese retention rates---80--92\\% of within-model accuracy preserved across continuation-mode pairs---suggest that a large component of the depth signal is text-intrinsic rather than tied to a specific generator.\n\nSecond, Qwen-rewrite exhibits catastrophic transfer failure.\nClassifiers trained on Qwen-rewrite retain only 61--63\\% of\nwithin-model accuracy when evaluated on Pythia or OLMo\n(45--50\\% by $\\tau$).\nThe reverse direction transfers asymmetrically: Pythia/OLMo${\\to}$Qwen-rewrite retains 70--72\\% of within-model accuracy, higher than the forward 61--63\\% but still far below continuation-mode transfer rates (80--92\\%).\nThis confirms that rewrite-mode classifiers rely on generation\nartifacts that do not generalize across models, in contrast\nto Qwen-continuation, which achieves 80--85\\% retention to the same targets.\n\nAt the 0v1 boundary, cross-model pairwise $\\auc$ exceeds 0.96\nfor all model pairs, confirming that the human-vs.-synthetic\ndistinction is consistently transferable across all tested generators\nand modes.\nThe transfer degradation concentrates at deeper boundaries\n(1v2 and beyond), where model- and mode-specific factors dominate.\n\nThe aggregate picture is one of modest cross-model loss:\nmean pairwise $\\auc$ is 0.6099 within-model versus 0.5905\ncross-model.\nThis gap is driven primarily by Qwen-rewrite; among\ncontinuation-mode pairs the within/cross difference narrows\nfurther.\n\n%% ====================================================================\n\\subsection{Feature Degradation Ordering}\n\\label{sec:feature_degradation}\n\nThe transfer results indicate that depth signals are largely text-intrinsic.\nWe next ask what drives discrimination: if the signal is text-intrinsic, different generators should rely on the same features in the same order.\nWe test this by computing single-feature $\\auc$ rankings\nindependently for each model and comparing them via rank\ncorrelation.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Top-3 features by single-feature $\\auc$ ranking for each\nmodel configuration.\nFeatures shared across all three continuation-mode models are in bold.\nQwen-rewrite's top feature (\\texttt{freq\\_entropy}) is mode-specific.\nSpearman $\\rho$ (continuation-mode pairs): Pythia--OLMo 0.95,\nPythia--Qwen-continuation 0.9143, OLMo--Qwen-continuation 0.85.}\n\\label{tab:top_features}\n\\begin{tabular}{cllll}\n\\toprule\nRank & Pythia & OLMo & Qwen-rw & Qwen-cont \\\\\n\\midrule\n1\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_d1\\_std}}\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_std}}\n  & \\texttt{freq\\_entropy}\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_d1\\_std}} \\\\\n2\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_d2\\_std}}\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_d1\\_std}}\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_std}}\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_std}} \\\\\n3\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_std}}\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_d2\\_std}}\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_d1\\_std}}\n  & \\textbf{\\texttt{surp\\_d2\\_std}} \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\nAmong continuation-mode models, single-feature $\\auc$ rankings\nare highly correlated:\nPythia vs.\\ OLMo $\\rho = 0.95$,\nPythia vs.\\ Qwen-continuation $\\rho = 0.9143$,\nOLMo vs.\\ Qwen-continuation $\\rho = 0.85$\n(all $\\rho \\geq 0.85$).\nThe top-3 features---\\texttt{surp\\_d1\\_std}, \\texttt{surp\\_std},\nand \\texttt{surp\\_d2\\_std}---are shared (up to ordering) across\nall three continuation-mode configurations\n(Table~\\ref{tab:top_features}).\nAll three measure variability of surprisal or its derivatives,\ncapturing the \\emph{heterogeneity} of token-level predictability\nacross the text.\nThat these features degrade in a shared order across generators\nsuggests a common mechanism: each generation step smooths the\ntoken-level surprisal landscape, reducing its variance in a\nmodel-independent fashion.\n\nQwen-rewrite diverges: its top-ranked feature is\n\\texttt{freq\\_entropy}, a lexical diversity measure absent from\nthe continuation-mode top-3 for any model.\nThis corroborates the transfer results in\nSection~\\ref{sec:transfer}---rewrite-mode classifiers learn\npartly from instruction-following artifacts rather than the\nshared surprisal-based signals.\n\nKendall $\\tau$ provides a complementary, more conservative\nperspective:\nPythia vs.\\ OLMo $\\tau = 0.733$,\nPythia vs.\\ Qwen-continuation $\\tau = 0.695$,\nOLMo vs.\\ Qwen-continuation $\\tau = 0.543$.\nCross-mode correlations are lower:\nPythia vs.\\ Qwen-rewrite $\\tau = 0.467$,\nOLMo vs.\\ Qwen-rewrite $\\tau = 0.429$.\n\n\\paragraph{Limitation.}\nThe high Spearman $\\rho$ characterizes the \\emph{aggregate}\nfeature ranking, but at the depth-pair level the agreement\nweakens: $\\tau \\approx 0.37$ at the 1v2 boundary.\nThis means that while the overall importance ordering is shared,\nthe relative contribution of individual features at the\ndiscrimination boundary---where precision matters most---varies\nacross models.\nWe view this as a genuine limitation of the text-intrinsic\ninterpretation and a direction for future investigation.\n\n%% ====================================================================\n\\subsection{Generation Mode Effect}\n\\label{sec:generation_mode}\n",

2. "+21.5--25.6pp over the 16.7% random baseline for 6-class classification"


3. "+5.2--9.3pp over LLM zero-shot at ~33%; cf. Table S3"

Now I'm checking the comparative performance claims: ordinal logistic regression gets the lowest MAE but struggles with pairwise AUC, while multiclass LightGBM matches OBD on AUC but underperforms on accuracy and MAE. Overall, OBD emerges as the best trade-off across these metrics. Now I'm looking at the performance metrics for exp_010 across all three models to verify the accuracy claims. Qwen-rewrite leads at 42.29%, followed by Pythia at 38.56% and OLMo at 38.22%, which matches the stated range of 38.2--42.3%. I'm checking the improvement over the random baseline next.

The improvements check out: 38.2 minus 16.7 gives 21.5 percentage points, and 42.3 minus 16.7 gives 25.6 percentage points. But now I'm noticing a potential issue with the LLM zero-shot comparison—the OBD results are from a 6-class task while the LLM baseline from exp_011 is 5-class, so these aren't directly comparable. I'm checking the actual metrics more carefully—exp_011 has an OBD 5-class accuracy of 38.6%, which means I can directly compare it to the LLM's ~33%, giving the 5.2–9.3 percentage point improvement the paper claims. 5-class task. So the paper SHOULD compare this 0.386 (38.6%) with LLM's ~33%, giving +5.5pp. But the paragraph compares the 6-class OBD range (38.2--42.3%) with LLM 5-class (~33%). The 38.2% is 6-class on OLMo (harder task), being compared to 33% from 5-class (easier task).

This is a **numerical mismatch across different classification granularities**. The paragraph conflates 6-class OBD accuracy with 5-class LLM accuracy. The correct comparison should use the 5-class OBD accuracy (38.6% from exp_011) vs 5-class LLM (~33%), giving +5.5pp. Alternatively, if using 6-class OBD, the LLM should also be tested on 6-class, but it was only evaluated on 5-class.

Now I'm checking the MAE values for ordinal logistic regression versus OBD on Qwen-rewrite—the registry shows 0.9105 versus 0.9836, which round correctly to 0.911 and 0.984. For the pairwise AUC claim, I'm verifying the mean across the ordinal logistic regression results. Looking at the performance comparisons across all three models, multiclass LightGBM edges out OBD slightly on pairwise AUC for two of the models but falls behind on the third, while OBD consistently outperforms on both accuracy and MAE across the board. I'm identifying a key issue that needs to be flagged.

The "+5.2--9.3pp over LLM zero-shot" claim is problematic because it's comparing OBD's 6-class accuracy against the LLM's 5-class accuracy—these are fundamentally different tasks with different random baselines (16.7% vs 20%). When I adjust for a fair comparison using OBD's 5-class baseline from the earlier experiment (38.6%) against the LLM's 5-class performance (~33%), the actual improvement drops to around +5.5pp. I'm also double-checking whether the Qwen-cont model appears in the earlier experiment's metrics, and it doesn't seem to be there, which raises questions about where that OBD result is coming from.

Looking more carefully at the experimental setup, I see there are actually four model configurations being tested (Qwen-rewrite, Pythia, OLMo, and Qwen-cont), but the earlier experiment only included three of them. The Qwen-cont ordinal accuracy comes from a different experiment, and when I include all four configurations, the OBD accuracy range becomes 38.19--42.29%, which rounds to the 38.2--42.3% cited in the paper. But the paper says "across all three model families" when there are actually four configurations involved here.

The bigger issue I'm noticing is that the LLM comparison is mixing different classification granularities, and I should check whether the Method section actually discusses the ordinal comparison approach. I'm noticing the ordinal logistic regression actually performs below chance at 0.4883 mean pairwise AUC, which is worse than random—though the paper's "near-chance" characterization is technically accurate, it downplays how poorly the method performs on this metric. The real issue worth flagging is the comparison between 5-class and 6-class models, and I should check whether the paragraph explicitly mentions the specific experiment being referenced. The key issue is that the 5-class versus 6-class comparison isn't fair—I should be comparing the 5-class OBD baseline from exp_011 (38.6%) against the LLM zero-shot accuracy instead, which would give a more accurate "+4.6--5.7pp" range. The exp_010 numbers check out correctly (38.22--42.29% across the three datasets), and the paragraph's analysis of the tradeoffs between ordinal and multiclass approaches is sound. Qwen-cont from exp_017 has ordinal_accuracy of 0.3819 = 38.19%, which is actually the lowest. If we include all 4 configurations, the range is 38.19--42.29%. The paper says "38.2--42.3%" which rounds correctly from the exp_010 range but omits the even-lower Qwen-cont. This is minor.

Another issue: the paragraph says "Across all three model families". But should it be "all four model configurations" to match the rest of the paper's framing? The paper uses 4 configurations. I think "three model families" is technically correct (Qwen, Pythia, OLMo) since exp_010 was run on 3 datasets, and the OBD metrics match for those 3. But it's a bit inconsistent with the rest of the section.

The main finding worth raising is the 5-class vs 6-class issue. The paragraph says "i.e., $+$21.5--25.6\,pp over the 16.7\% random baseline for 6-class classification". It correctly identifies this as 6-class. Then says "and $+$5.2--9.3\,pp over LLM zero-shot at ${\sim}$33\%; cf.\ Table~\ref{tab:s3_llm_zeroshot}". But 33% is the 5-class LLM accuracy, while the "+5.2--9.3pp" is computed from 6-class OBD accuracy minus 5-class LLM accuracy. The reader would naturally interpret this as the same classification task, which it isn't. I see the issue now—the 6-class task actually has a lower random baseline (16.7%) compared to the 5-class task (20%), which means OBD is tackling a harder problem. When I normalize both results relative to their respective baselines, OBD still comes out ahead, so the comparison actually strengthens rather than weakens OBD's performance, even though comparing across different task granularities is methodologically problematic.

The right fix is to either compare OBD and LLM on the same classification scheme (5-class OBD at 38.6% versus 5-class LLM at 33%, a +5.6pp difference), have the LLM attempt the 6-class task, or report both results with their baselines clearly stated rather than just the raw percentage point difference. This is a warning-level issue because a reviewer could flag it and damage the paper's credibility on numerical accuracy.

I should also check the MAE claim—calling OBD's MAE "competitive" when it's 0.984 compared to ordinal logistic regression's 0.911 is overstated, though in the context of comparing against multiclass LightGBM's 1.016, it does look better.

Now looking at the pairwise AUC claim more carefully: multiclass LightGBM actually outperforms OBD on two of the three models (Qwen-rewrite and Pythia), so saying multiclass "matches" OBD is misleading—it's actually ahead in most cases.

I've identified two key issues: the 5-class versus 6-class comparison is fundamentally misleading, and that's the main concern worth flagging. The other metrics check out. Time to write up the findings.

I already have the exp_010 and exp_011 data from my first registry read. Let me verify the key claim that's problematic.

From exp_010 registry:
- OBD accuracy: Qwen-rw 42.29%, Pythia 38.56%, OLMo 38.22% → range 38.2–42.3% ✓
- Random baseline for 6-class: 16.7% → +21.5–25.6pp ✓

From exp_011 registry:
- GPT-5.5 5-class acc: 0.332 = 33.2%


- Claude 5-class acc: 0.331 = 33.1%
- OBD baseline 5-class acc: 0.386 = 38.6%

I see the issue now—the paper's calculation uses 38.2–42.3% (the 6-class OBD results) against 33% (the 5-class LLM results), which are incomparable. The proper comparison on the same 5-class task shows OBD at 38.6% versus LLMs around 33%, giving a +5.5pp gap instead.

Let me check if Appendix Table S3 provides any additional clarity on this discrepancy.

_source line 74 · 2026-05-14T08:50:55.920Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I can see the key information. The Appendix Table S3 caption explicitly states:

"${\sim}0.33$ vs.\ OBD-LightGBM baseline 0.386, evaluated on the full test set $N{=}5{,}000$"

And the LLM experiment is 5-class: "5×5 confusion matrices" (depths 0-4).

So in the Appendix, the comparison IS done correctly: 5-class LLM (~33%) vs 5-class OBD baseline (38.6%) = +5.5pp.


But the main text has a different framing—it reports OBD's 38.2–42.3% range as a 6-class result, which is where the larger improvement margins come from.

The issue is that the "+5.2–9.3pp" improvement over LLM is mixing different classification setups: the 6-class OBD numbers are being compared against the 5-class LLM baseline of ~33%, which isn't a fair comparison. The correct approach would be to either compare 5-class OBD (38.6%) against 5-class LLM for a +5.5pp improvement, or stick with 6-class OBD but find the corresponding 6-class LLM baseline instead.

The appendix table S3 actually does this comparison correctly with matching granularities, so the main text is contradicting its own appendix. This feels like a careless oversight rather than intentional misrepresentation, though it does artificially inflate the advantage range.

Now I'm checking whether the claim that multiclass LightGBM consistently underperforms holds up across the experiments—looking at the accuracy and MAE metrics from exp_010 to verify this pattern. The pairwise AUC differences are minimal across models, so calling it a match is reasonable. Let me check the GPU status for the running experiment before I start writing the report.
```

</details>
